B1701 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0016 | def infer_single_site_substitution(comp_a: str, comp_b: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
B1702 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0017 |     """Infer the one-element substitution needed to turn ``comp_a`` into ``comp_b``."""
B1703 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0018 |     a = Composition(comp_a).fractional_composition
B1704 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0019 |     b = Composition(comp_b).fractional_composition
B1705 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0020 |     elements = sorted({str(el) for el in a.elements} | {str(el) for el in b.elements})
B1706 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0021 |     removed: list[str] = []
B1707 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0022 |     added: list[str] = []
B1708 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0023 |     for element in elements:
B1709 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0024 |         delta = b.get_atomic_fraction(element) - a.get_atomic_fraction(element)
B1710 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0025 |         if delta < -1e-8:
B1711 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0026 |             removed.append(element)
B1712 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0027 |         elif delta > 1e-8:
B1713 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0028 |             added.append(element)
B1714 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0029 |     if len(removed) != 1 or len(added) != 1:
B1715 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0030 |         raise ValueError(f"expected a single-site substitution, got {comp_a!r} -> {comp_b!r}")
B1716 src/ssscreen/stability/sqs.py:0031 |     return removed[0], added[0]
